+ "!stream.retryTimer"
+ "%@ has %lu characteristic requests that cannot be handled"

+ ", recording: %@"

+ ", triggerType: %lu"
+ ", videoSensorUUID: %@"
+ "-seed"
+ "<%@: %p, sensorUUID=%@, zones=%@, included=%@, triggers=%@>"
+ "<no xpc policy>"
+ "<not supported>"
